Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cell_biology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 807 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 625 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 807 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cell_biology structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 625. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
